STATUS DEFINITIONS
authored_unreviewed: the assessed section extent is supplied; independent correctness/source/publication acceptance is not established.
partial: substantive writing exists, with recorded writing/proof/example/source-presentation or scope obligations remaining.
missing: the recorded assessed intervals did not supply the target treatment; later drafts can supersede that negative observation.
unassessed: relevant whole-section extent is unread/unmatched in the ledger; this is not proof of absent prose.

Those on-sheet apparatus obligations remain separate from the prose lookup.
  Recorded remaining duty: The literal lk and lk-defneg sheets do not themselves link the presentation-equivalence theorem required by A-003/C03; the current chapter does link it. This is an on-sheet writing residual, not an equivalence-proof verdict.
